Report per-task and overall success rate\\n\\nBaselines:\\n  - \\\"vla_only\\\": SmolVLA mean of K=10, no CRM refinement\\n  - \\\"crm_scalar\\\": CRM with scalar uncertainty (single std, not per-DOF)\\n  - \\\"crm_perdof\\\": CRM with full per-DOF uncertainty (ours)\\n  - \\\"acr_vla\\\": alias for crm_perdof — the full ACR-VLA pipeline\\n  - \\\"reconvla\\\": ReconVLA baseline — scalar Euclidean argmin selection (plan_014)\\n  - \\\"adaptive_k\\\": Adaptive K — K=2 L2 probe, expand to K=10 + CRM obs_only if uncertain\\n\\nAdaptive K mode (--mode adaptive_k):\\n  Each step: generate K=2 samples, compute pairwise L2 distance.\\n  If L2 > threshold (high uncertainty): generate 8 more (total K=10),\\n    select argmin(L2 to mean), apply CRM obs_only correction (epsilon=0.2).\\n  If       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"precompute-pi05-goal-2048-r2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Precomputing π0.5 obs_features and K=2048 action samples on LIBERO-Goal enables downstream CRM training for cross-model (SmolVLA→π0.5) generalization verification without re-running inference at train time.\"}, \"eval-supp-long-vla-only-s46\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Long VLA-only baseline seed46. Fifth seed for Long VLA-only variance analysis. 3-seed mean 58.0%±1.5%.\", \"metrics\": {\"success_rate\": 60.5, \"successes\": 121, \"total\": 200, \"source\": \"reviewer_manual_check\"}}, \"precompute-pi05-spatial-2048-r2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Precomputing π0.5 obs_features and K=2048 action samples on LIBERO-Spatial enables downstream CRM training for cross-model (SmolVLA→π0.5) generalization verification without re-running inference at train time.\"}, \"precompute-smolvla-goal-bjb1-32546\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Precompute SmolVLA obs_features + K=10 action samples on LIBERO-Goal for unified 4-suite CRM training\"}, \"eval-supp-goal-vla-only-s46\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Goal VLA-only baseline seed46. Tests whether same pattern holds on Spatial: if Random ≈ VLA-only (~29.6%), CRM's +5pp is from guidance not sampling diversity.\", \"metrics\": {\"success_rate\": 34, \"successes\": 34, \"total\": 100}}, \"eval-adaptive-k-spatial-p90-seed44-bjb2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Adaptive K with τ=P90 (0.125870) on LIBERO-Spatial seed44. All 6 GPUs deployed and running (each at ~6.5GB / 12-22% util):

## 部署结果

**关键发现**：原 task 列出的 4 个 spatial s45/s46 实验**已全部完成**（见 `eval_spatial_*seed45/seed46.json`）。registry 中也已记录 DONE。CoRL 5-seed 矩阵 (Spatial / Goal / Object) 基本完整，**剩余真正空缺只在 Long s46**——4 个原本 RUNNING 的实验在 bjb1-32546 死于 `python: command not found`（conda 未激活），从未产生 JSON。

**6 GPU 部署清单（全部 cqa1-32758，Long 套件）**：

| GPU | tmux session | Exp ID | mode | seed |
|-----|-----|------|------|------|
| 0 | long-adapt-s46-r2 | eval-supp-long-adapt-tau025-s46-r2 | adaptive_conformal_crm τ=0.25 | 46 |
| 1 | long-crm-s46-r2 | eval-supp-long-crm-norm005-s46-r2 | crm_obs_only norm=0.05 | 46 |
| 2 | long-reconvla-s46-r2 | eval-supp-long-reconvla-s46-r2 | reconvla K=10 | 46 |
| 3 | long-k2mean-s46-r2 | eval-supp-long-k2mean-s46-r2 | vla_only K=2 mean | 46 |
| 4 | long-adapt-s47 | eval-supp-long-adapt-tau025-s47 | adaptive_conformal_crm τ=0.25 | 47 (6-seed) |
| 5 | long-vla-s47 | eval-supp-long-vla-only-s47 | vla_only K=2 mean | 47 (6-seed) |

**Registry 状态**：4 个旧 RUNNING entries 已 conclusion=failed；6 个新 entries 已 PENDING→QUEUED→ALLOCATED→RUNNING（带完整 spec）。

**Step 4 (reconvla obj 收割)**：cuda:2/cuda:4 在我连入时已无 tmux session（任务在 ssh-disconnect 后或自然结束时清理掉了），registry 中 `eval-supp-reconvla-obj-s45/s46` 已是 DONE 状态。无需额外收割。

预计 5-6 小时全部完成（Long max_steps=520，每实验 ~10×20=200 rollouts）。
